## IORIO Signal Engine — probabilistic trade-scoring proposal (research phase complete 2026-09-16; Phase 0 data foundation started 2026-09-21)

Marcelo's ultimate goal: remove human execution from CC/CSP trading entirely, with Genosuke (or similar) placing/closing/rolling orders and IORIO Pulse as the monitoring/override layer. Before touching execution, he asked for a rigorous, literature-backed probabilistic model to rank which alerts are actually worth trading — "maximum profit, minimum risk over an infinite timeline," with a risk throttle that need not scale reward linearly. Also asked whether expanding to SPX/XSP/SPY is worthwhile.

**Research basis, findings and proposed architecture: see the guiding artifact linked at the top of this file (§01–§12).** The earlier copy of that text lives in `PROGRESS-ARCHIVE.md`.


**Decisions made 2026-09-16** (via AskUserQuestion, all four "recommended" options chosen):
1. Default risk-throttle starting point: **quarter-Kelly** (conservative, given the VRP-decline finding above).
2. Build order: **read-only scoring/ranking dashboard in IORIO Pulse first** — no execution authority — before any sizing or automation work.
3. SPX/XSP/SPY: **deferred** until the single-name scoring model is validated.
4. Directional tilt (momentum + options-skew): **included from day one** alongside the volatility-edge model (the one decision where Marcelo went against the "defer it" recommendation — build it in from the start, not as a later phase).

- **Built 2026-09-21 (the parts needing no IBKR; uncommitted, not deployed)**: migration `20260921000001_create_option_chain_snapshots.ts` (`option_chain_snapshots` + `option_quote_snapshots`, exactly as approved plus a `contracts_with_any_tick` column for the approved starved-ticker rule); `src/lib/optionChainCaptureWindow.ts` (Formula 7: `computeStrikeWindow`, `selectContractsToCapture`); `src/lib/optionChainCaptureClock.ts` (`isWithinChainCaptureClockWindow`, reuses `easternInstant`/`easternDateIso` from `marketSessionStatus.ts`); `src/lib/realizedVolatility.ts` (approved Yang-Zhang + split guard: ±70% rule, ratio detector confirmed by volume, ≥5 prior bars needed to check volume else conservatively flagged). Parameters approved as listed: split detector ±15% of 1/m for m∈{2,3,4,5,10,20}, volume ≥ 0.5·m × prior-20-bar median; ~60 lines/batch; starved = <90% of contracts with any tick, one re-capture, only if <45 min elapsed, 10-min cap; guard 10:00–10:30 ET.
- **Verified 2026-09-21**: 36 new tests + full suite 133/133, `tsc --noEmit` clean. Yang-Zhang checked against an **independent Python implementation** (every component to 12–15 digits), against a large seeded simulation with known true vol (no drift and 150%/yr drift, within 6%), and **mutation-tested** (dropping the overnight term or swapping the RS weight makes 3 tests fail; file restored). Clock guard tested at all EDT/EST boundaries, both DST switch days, and **every day of 2026–2027: exactly one of the 14:00/15:00 UTC Scheduler slots passes the guard, even with a 2-minute start delay**. Migration applied to local dev DB only (Batch 30), verified directly: write/read-back, unique(ticker, day), option_right CHECK, contract PK, delete-header-cascades-to-contracts then re-insert (the approved replace-on-re-run), same contract stored across days, all rolled back to 0 rows; rollback and re-apply both clean. Prod gets the migration on the next deploy (release phase) after Marcelo pushes.
- **Real-data check of the Yang-Zhang engine** (`tmp/checkYangZhangOnRealBars.ts`, read-only, dev DB): plausible on all 23 tickers (e.g. TLT ~10%, CSCO ~25–38%, LITX ~176–203%), no split flags anywhere in the last year. **Observation, not a bug**: on trending names (COIN 77 vs 98, HOOD 74 vs 93, IGV 30 vs 40 for YZ21 vs close-to-close 21-day) Yang-Zhang reads well below close-to-close, because its range term treats a steady intraday move as drift (RS < open-close on those names) and it assumes overnight and intraday moves are independent — in those windows they point the same way. It measures diffusive vol; the spread of a multi-day option's terminal price also depends on trend, so the richness comparison (Phase 1/2) should consider close-to-close alongside it — a forecast-model decision for later, formula unchanged. Guard behaved correctly on NBIS's 2022 Yandex-era history: 2022-02-24 (open 0.527× prior close on 66.5M shares ≈ 10× normal volume — the Russia-invasion crash, the accepted "crash looks like a split" false positive) and 2022-03-24 (zero-volume trading-suspension artifact, 8.00 → 18.94, caught by the ±70% rule). SMCI's Oct-2024 10-for-1 split can't be checked until the 5-year backfill exists.
- **Also built 2026-09-21, second pass (offline-verified, uncommitted)**: `src/ibkr/captureOptionQuoteBatch.ts` — the archive's quote collector (streaming `reqMktData` with generic tick 101; captures bid/ask/last, bid/ask sizes, open interest, volume, IBKR model IV + greeks, model price and the spot IBKR used; real-time and delayed tick types both accepted; IBKR "no data" sentinels normalized to null; errors recorded per contract; every subscription cancelled and every listener removed). **A new module, not a change to `fetchQuotesForContracts`**, because alerts and the Ticker Detail chain depend on that one — an implementation choice made without asking (fully reversible; Marcelo can veto and have it merged instead). `src/lib/optionChainCaptureCoverage.ts` (approved constants: 60-line batches, starved <90% any-tick, re-capture only inside 45 min / 10-min cap; `splitIntoBatches`, coverage, status complete/partial/failed, market-data-type derivation, re-capture rule). `src/lib/optionChainSnapshotStore.ts` (`saveOptionChainSnapshot`: same-day re-run replaces, one transaction, chunked inserts, duplicate contracts collapse to the last). Verification: 27 new tests (collector tested against a **fake IBKR feed**: real-time, delayed, sentinels, ceiling, errors, cleanup, foreign req ids) — full suite 160/160, `tsc` clean; the writer round-tripped against the real dev DB (read-back, same-day replace with 0 orphans, 4,000-contract chunked insert, cleanup). **Live-verified 2026-09-21 (pre-market probe, see below)**: open interest, bid/ask sizes and volume arrive per contract in streaming mode (tickSize 27/28, 0/3, 8) under generic tick 101. **Still unverified (only flows during market hours)**: model computation ticks 13/83 (IV, greeks, model price, spot used) and real bid/ask prices — the probe must be repeated at the open.
- **Verification pass 2026-09-21 (Marcelo: "test and double check everything, check sources")** — *Sources checked*: IBKR's tick-types docs confirm every tick type used (sizes 0/3/5/8, open interest 27/28 via `tickSize` under generic tick 101, delayed 66/67/68/69/70/74, computation 10–13 and delayed 80–83). The `@stoqey/ib` v1.6.6 decoder already turns IBKR's "not computed" markers into `undefined` (IV −1, delta −2, optPrice −1, pvDividend −1, gamma/vega/theta −2, undPrice −1) — my null handling is a second layer on top; a theta of exactly −2 can never be seen (library drops it). IBKR docs: **50 messages/second from the client side, shared by all clients on a Gateway**, and **100 market-data lines per TWS *username*, combined across TWS and every API app on that login** (not per connection); the library has its own limiter (default 40 msg/s, 4 per 100 ms), so a 60-contract batch costs ~3 s of subscribe+cancel messaging before any data. `option_chain_params` (cached 24 h) returns a **union of strikes across expiries** — the job must validate each (expiry, strike) with `getCachedValidStrikes`, and the existing code fires those checks with `Promise.all`: fine for dozens, **not for thousands on the first night — the job needs a concurrency cap** (message limit). *Two real flaws found in my own code and fixed*: open-interest ticks (27/28) were counted as "real-time" evidence, which would mislabel delayed data as "mixed" (removed; static daily data says nothing about the feed); and the fallback request-id counter was per call, so two concurrent batches on one one-shot socket could collide (now module-level). *Test rigour*: 12 deliberate breakages of the new modules ("mutations") — 10 caught immediately, **2 survived, exposing 2 real test gaps** (no quote with a bid but no ask; no call and put at the same strike), both closed and re-run: all 12 now caught. Suite 163/163, `tsc` clean. Live probe `tmp/liveOptionCollectorProbe.ts` (typecheck caught that `selectContractsToCapture` returns strike+side only — the job must attach the expiry) **run with Marcelo's permission, 2026-09-21 04:34 ET (pre-market), AMAT Oct-2 expiry, 9 contracts, one batch, read-only** — and it found a **real bug the docs didn't reveal**: for every option contract IBKR sends BOTH open-interest ticks, **tick 27 = open interest of CALLS, tick 28 = PUTS, and the tick for the other right is always 0**. My first version kept whichever arrived last, so every call's real OI (152, 3, 83, 45 on AMAT 445/447.5/450/452.5C) was overwritten with 0 (looked implausible for a liquid name). Fixed: a call reads tick 27, a put tick 28, in either arrival order; new test proven to fail against the old behavior; re-probed and confirmed. Other live observations: pre-market, bid/ask arrive as −1 (→ null, sentinel handling works), sizes and volume arrive as 0, **no computation ticks at all** (no IV/greeks without quotes, so `settled` never triggers and the batch runs to its ceiling — expected off-hours), plus tick 9 (previous close) and tick 5 (last size), and several ticks arrive twice per contract. Batch of 9 subscribes took the full 15 s ceiling (nothing to settle on).

SPY/QQQ are not in `tickers` yet (dev DB). Adding to the real shortlist is Marcelo's action via the UI.

- **First real pipeline run + hang found and fixed, 2026-09-21.** SMCI (paper Gateway, market closed): 64 s, 1,253 bars + 1,253 IV points stored (2021-09-22 → 2026-09-18), 2 earnings events, 237 contracts / 8 expiries validated, first snapshot skipped (market closed). IBKR's fresh 5Y bars come back split-adjusted (no gap at SMCI's Oct-2024 10:1 split), so the split guard could not be exercised on real data. **Hang:** the test script never exited because `fetchLivePrices` borrows the lazy, process-lifetime `sharedReadConnection`; a Scheduler job doing that would never finish. Fixed with `sharedReadConnection.shutdown()` (called in the capture script's `finally`); verified the probe now exits by itself. The web dyno is unaffected.
- **SIGNALS SCREEN: mockup submitted 2026-09-22 (not approved, nothing built).** Marcelo's request: a NEW screen 'Signals' (all shortlist tickers, real-time) + a new Signals ticker modal that identifies and grades trades on the option chain in real time + a reworked order-setup flow; Trade Alerts stays untouched (the new system will take a while to test). Decisions so far: headline = Net Edge (vol points) + Edge $ per contract (= net Edge × vega × 100, formula not yet signed off); grades = four tiers cut from each ticker's own live net-Edge distribution (Strong top 10%, Good next 20%, Marginal next 30%, Avoid rest; net Edge ≤ 0 is always Avoid); candidates = STRUCTURAL filters only (OTM side, two-sided quote, ≥1 day, quality-gated slice), no spread/delta/DTE/OI cuts, Avoid tier hidden behind a toggle; trades you cannot place (no shares / no cash) are shown, scored and flagged with Sell disabled; a per-ticker and global 'not accounted for' roadmap with ETAs (UncompensatedShare/Tilt not blended, risk-adjusted ratio, skew/momentum validation, earnings adjustment, dividends, SVI stability, friction λ, sizing) as Marcelo's reminder list of what to wire in later. Design: live spot for every row (existing price stream), live option quotes only for the open modal's candidates (line-budget aware), surface stays the 10:00 snapshot (sticky-moneyness). Order setup gains a Signal card, a Limit-at control (Mid/Between/Bid) that recomputes net Edge, a live decay meter, and saves the signal snapshot with the order for Phase 2. Artifact: https://claude.ai/artifact/56mJsUpZEXjGJ8YT3dLkvD
- **SIGNALS SCREEN: mockup APPROVED 2026-09-22 (Marcelo: "looks good"). Stage 1 of 5 built (see next entry).** Final mockup: https://claude.ai/artifact/Gjj4J1uTCLss38zF2dWaae (v3; supersedes the earlier https://claude.ai/artifact/56mJsUpZEXjGJ8YT3dLkvD link, which this session lost write access to mid-conversation). Revisions from the first draft, all applied: ticker/name split into two columns; flag badges kept in the opportunity table (footnote removed) and also spelled out with their full explanation in the order panel's Contract IV card; Bid/Ask replaced with Spread % in the opportunity list only (chain grid keeps bid/ask); orders are Adaptive (Patient/Normal/Urgent), not limit — the Signal card shows net Edge at the mid (best case) and at the bid (worst case, how the list scores it), since Adaptive has no single fixed fill price; modal header/Positions/Wheel-cycle card match the real Ticker Detail modal, chart + IV history moved to the bottom. Two things explained to Marcelo, not yet formula changes: momentum is a trailing 12-1 month window (not calendar-year YTD), skip-month is from Jegadeesh & Titman 1993 (short-term reversal), not my own choice; Edge $ = net Edge × vega × 100 does not also multiply by option/share price because vega (∂price/∂σ) already carries the price level — multiplying by price too would double-count it. Edge $ is MY OWN construction, not from a cited paper — **Marcelo signed it off 2026-09-22** ("Edge $ formula: approved"); it now ranks the screen's headline. Mockup v3 (2026-09-22): the modal's chain grid had Puts on the left and Calls on the right, the reverse of the real Ticker Detail modal — Marcelo caught it from a side-by-side screenshot; fixed to Calls left / Puts right with explicit "Calls"/"Puts" group headers, same as `TickerDetailModal.tsx`. The scoring code itself (`signalCandidates.ts`, OTM filter) was never affected — mockup-only bug.
- **SIGNALS SCREEN stage 1 of 5 BUILT + VERIFIED 2026-09-22 (backend data layer, read-only, snapshot-based; committed 2026-09-23, api `532bc8e`).** Plan Marcelo approved: 1 backend → 2 live layer (spot for every row via the price stream, option quotes only for the open modal, line-budget aware) → 3 Signals screen → 4 Signals modal → 5 order-setup changes (Signal card, Adaptive priority, decay meter, save the signal snapshot with the order). Built: `src/lib/signalCandidates.ts` (pure: `buildSignalCandidates` → structural filters only, delta + mid-IV computed from the fitted surface, never read from IBKR's tick 13; `gradeSignalCandidates` → four tiers at the 90/70/40 quantiles of the ticker's own net Edge, net Edge ≤ 0 always Avoid; `pickBestCandidate` → max Edge $, ties by net Edge), `blackScholesDelta` added to `impliedVolatilitySurface.ts` (checked against an independent Python reference), `src/lib/signalsStore.ts` (`loadTickerSignals` / `loadSignalsScreen`: latest complete-or-partial snapshot → `option_surface_fits` slices + `option_quote_snapshots` → forecast as of the snapshot date (no lookahead) → candidates; momentum/elevated flag/skew/next earnings alongside; free shares via `fetchAvailableUncoveredShares`, free cash = IBKR TotalCashValue − cash locked in open CSPs, fetched ONCE per screen load), `src/routes/signals.ts` (`GET /signals`, `GET /signals/:symbol`, session-auth, 404 for a symbol not on the shortlist). Tests: `signalCandidates.test.ts` 27 tests (suite now 381); 18-mutation sweep, 5 initial survivors → 2 got new boundary tests (OTM at exactly the forward; k exactly at kMin/kMax; Edge $ vs an independently computed vega) and 3 were equivalent mutants already caught downstream (ask == bid and 0-DTE both die in `computeFrictionCost`'s guards) — kept the explicit guards for readability, documented in-code. Real-data check (dev DB, 20 shortlist tickers, 3.2 s): AAOI (only ticker with a fitted surface) → 333 candidates, all Avoid (forecast 124.6% ≫ surface IV ~87–107%, as established earlier), all four flags fire (`wide_spread` 34, `no_shares` 174 = every covered call, `outside_fitted_range` 31, `spans_earnings` 38), sample candidate's edge/net-Edge/Edge $ arithmetic checked by hand; the other 19 → `unscored=no_snapshot` with momentum/elevated flag still populated from bars. `GET /signals/aaoi` (lower-case, case-folded) returns the byte-identical object to the screen row; 404 and 401 paths verified. Not in stage 1 by design: `dayChangePercent` is `null` until stage 2. `fetchAccountSummary` took the documented one-shot fallback once when the shared read connection wasn't ready in time — no hang, no error.
- **SIGNALS SCREEN stage 2 of 5 BUILT + VERIFIED 2026-09-22 (live layer, backend; committed 2026-09-23, api `532bc8e`).** Plain-language: the Signals numbers now follow live prices. The 10:00 surface is re-read at the live spot (sticky moneyness: every expiry's forward scales with spot), so net Edge / Edge $ / grade / delta move on every tick; the screen gets one stock line per shortlist ticker, the open modal additionally gets live bid/ask for up to 40 contracts and the UncompensatedShare simulation refreshed in a worker thread. **Decisions (Marcelo, all this day):** (1) re-scoring runs SERVER-side over the existing stream multiplexer, decided only after a like-for-like benchmark of the identical file on the M4 laptop vs a Basic one-off dyno on `iorio-staging-api`: per-tick re-score of 333 contracts (SVI + Black-Scholes + IV bisection) 0.5 ms laptop / 1.5 ms dyno — a non-issue; UncompensatedShare Monte Carlo at 4000 paths 1.95 s / 4.00 s, at 1000 paths 0.45 s / 0.96 s (dyno ≈ 2× the laptop, not the order of magnitude feared); FE would halve the Monte Carlo but duplicate the approved formula in the app repo. (2) Monte Carlo: worker thread (`uncompensatedShareWorker.ts` + `uncompensatedShareWorkerPool.ts`, one worker per API process, never blocks the event loop; first `worker_threads` use in the repo — `.ts` worker under tsx needs `--import tsx` in execArgv for vitest, verified), **1000 paths** (noise ≈ 1 pp, the same size as the drift from a 1% spot move; 16-seed measurement: 4000→0.54 pp, 2000→0.76, 1000→1.01, 500→1.41, 250→2.12), **every 5 s but only after a ≥ 0.5% spot move** since the last simulation (sensitivity measured on AAOI: ~1 pp of timing share per 1% of spot, max ~2 pp), only for the open modal's ticker; `GET /signals/:symbol` uses 1000 paths too so the value doesn't jump on the first refresh from noise alone. (3) Day change vs `daily_price_bars`' last close strictly before today's Eastern date (no IBKR tick-9 line; null at snapshot prices — the 10:00 spot vs that day's close is not a day change, caught live and fixed). (4) At most 1 frame/s per subscription (frames carry full state — the multiplexer coalesces snapshot streams under back-pressure, so deltas are not an option). (5) Free cash / free shares refreshed every 60 s. (6) Live quotes: the selected expiry's OTM candidates first, then the top-20 by Edge $, capped at 40 lines, one right per strike (`fetchQuotesForContracts` directly, half of what `quoteOptionChain` would use), on the shared live connection; every candidate carries `quoteSource: "live" | "snapshot"` and all rank together. Known consequence: a dense expiry (AAOI 2026-10-16 has ≥ 40 OTM strikes) fills the cap alone, so no other expiry gets live quotes then. **Refactor forced by the benchmark:** the Monte Carlo is no longer inside `buildSignalCandidates` (the screen never shows it and would have paid 20 × 1.6 s per load) — `attachUncompensatedShare` fills it on demand; `GET /signals` went 3.2 s → 0.7 s and 192 KB → 13 KB (rows carry `best` + `gradeCounts`, never the candidate list); the loop in `uncompensatedShare.ts` now carries each step's after-state into the next (bit-identical output, verified on the exact AAOI $90-put fingerprint 43.7295952466648; 1.35× faster). **Built:** `signalsTypes.ts`, `signalsLiveScoring.ts` (pure: `scoreTicker`, `scaleSlicesToLiveSpot`, `mergeLiveQuotes`, `selectLiveQuoteContracts`, `shouldRefreshUncompensatedShare`, …), `signalsStore.ts` (DB loaders only, `loadTickerSignalsInputs` once per stream), `ibkr/streamSignalsOptionQuotes.ts`, `streams/signalsProducers.ts` (`signalsScreen` no params; `signalsTicker {symbol, expiry?}`; injectable dependencies), kinds registered in `streamProtocol.ts`/`streamProducers.ts`, `formatIsoDateAsExpiry` next to its inverse. Wire frames: `{type:"signalsScreen", at, rows}` and `{type:"signalsTicker", at, signals, liveQuoteContracts, uncompensatedAsOf}`. **Tests:** suite 381 → 422 (live scoring 21 incl. an independent SVI/Black-Scholes reference for the sticky-moneyness re-read; worker pool 3 incl. exact equality with the synchronous path; producers 8 with fake deps + fake timers: 1 s coalescing, frozen→live, 60 s account refresh flips executability, expiry-first contract selection, 5 s/0.5% Monte Carlo gating, 404). **Real run (dev server, 07:06 ET pre-market):** screen 26 frames/30 s, all 20 tickers live with day change; ticker stream: snapshot → Monte Carlo filled 0.5 s later → spot 106.14→108.51 dropped 12 calls that were no longer OTM → Monte Carlo re-ran at the new spot after the 5 s tick. **NOT YET TESTED — six open items (listed 2026-09-22 at Marcelo's request; stage 1 and 2 are verified except for these):** (1) ~~*Live option bid/ask with real numbers*~~ **CLOSED 2026-09-22 10:08 ET** in the stage-4 modal: 36 of 40 subscribed contracts live two-sided, 4 fell back to the snapshot quote (see the stage-4 entry). (2) ~~*Market-hours tick rates*~~ **CLOSED 2026-09-22 10:15 ET**: screen + ticker streams for 30 s with AAOI ticking live — 29 frames each in 29 s, never more than 1 frame per one-second bucket, minimum gap 999 ms. (3) *IBKR line budget with other screens open* — screen (20 lines) + ticker modal (≤ 41 lines) ran together once and worked, but not alongside the Ticker Detail modal's 96 lines, which would exceed IBKR's 100-line cap. This is a pre-existing gap (no central line counter exists anywhere in the codebase) that the new streams make more likely to hit. Close: a DESIGN DECISION (prevent Signals and Ticker Detail from being open together, or shrink Ticker Detail's chain budget) before the frontend makes opening both easy. (4) *Worker thread from compiled `dist/*.js`* — verified under tsx (dev) and vitest only; the `.ts`/`.js` path switch in `uncompensatedShareWorkerPool.ts` is reasoned, not executed. Close: the first staging deploy (a failure would be immediate and loud: every `GET /signals/:symbol` and ticker stream would log a worker error). (5) *Dyno CPU under real load* — the benchmark was a one-off dyno at 18:00, not the web dyno serving streams at 10:00 ET. Close: watch staging's dyno metrics with a modal open during market hours; the path count is a one-line constant if it needs lowering. (6) ~~*Unsubscribe cleanup*~~ **CLOSED at the client-observable level 2026-09-22 10:15 ET**: after unsubscribing both Signals streams (connection kept open) no frame arrived in the following 8 s; the IBKR `cancelMktData` calls run in the same `finally` path the existing Trade Alerts price stream relies on. Not observed directly: the Gateway's own line count after cancel (no way to read it from outside the worker).
- **SIGNALS SCREEN stage 3 of 5 BUILT + VERIFIED 2026-09-22 (the Signals screen, app repo; committed 2026-09-23, api `532bc8e` / app `b70efbd`).** Plain-language: there is now a "Signals" page in the menu showing every shortlist ticker with its live price, day change, best opportunity, grade, net Edge and Edge $, plus ATM IV, forecast volatility, momentum, the volatility flag, next earnings and surface status — all updating from the live stream — and a "not accounted for" chip per ticker. **Decisions (Marcelo):** roadmap ETAs are COMPUTED from real data counts (`GET /signals/roadmap`, `lib/signalsRoadmap.ts`): nightly chain snapshots (blending needs 126, skew validation 63, SVI stability 20), past earnings dates per reporting ticker (4 quarters; ETFs excluded from the minimum — caught live: SPY pinned it at 0 forever), Signals-order fills (0 until stage 5 creates the table, stated as such), projected at 5 trading days per 7 calendar days; decision/sign-off/later-phase items stay text. Per-ticker caveats come from the backend row (`caveats`): no snapshot, short history (momentum needs 253 bars, the own 90th-percentile threshold 377; a 252-bar backfill points at "Backfill history"), dividend payer. The mockup's "odd price history / suspected splits" caveat is NOT derivable (the split guard records nothing) — dropped for now and listed as a 9th roadmap item instead. Rows are inert until the stage-4 modal. **Backend additions:** `atmImpliedVolatility` (slice nearest 30 days at log-moneyness 0 — constant under sticky moneyness, so it does not move with spot), `forecast`, `dailyBarCount`, `hasDividendEvents`, `caveats` on every row; suite 424 → 433. **Frontend:** `api/signals.ts` (types + `openSignalsScreenStream`, no legacy route: an API without the multiplexer has no Signals either), `pages/SignalsPage.tsx`, `lib/signalsPresentation.ts` (every visible label has a plain explanation, used as header tooltips), `formatVolatilityPoints` in the shared formatters, `DataTable` gained generic `toolbar` / `beforeTable` / `afterTable` / `onRowClick` / `rowClassName` slots, stream kinds added to the multiplexer client, route `/signals` + nav entry. Grades use Tabler semantic badges (success / cyan / yellow / secondary), no custom colours. **Verified with Playwright against the live dev servers (market open, 07:40 ET):** 20 rows, AAOI scored (Put $90, Avoid, −21 vp, ATM IV 88.6% = the mockup's 88.5), 19 "Unscored · No chain snapshot yet", prices ticking with direction colour + flash, "Live prices connected" with the pulse dot, roadmap panel with real dates, caveat popover per ticker, column gear, light AND dark, 1440 px and 390 px (no horizontal overflow). Three bugs found and fixed during the visual pass: (1) the caveat popover was clipped by `.table-responsive`'s overflow for lower rows → positioned against the viewport; (2) it then closed itself within a second — the live re-render fires scroll events inside the table and a capture-phase scroll listener caught them → follows the button on page scroll instead, closes only when the button leaves the viewport; (3) on mobile it ran past the bottom of the screen → opens on whichever side has more room, height capped to that room. Console: one `ERR_INCOMPLETE_CHUNKED_ENCODING` on `/stream` = the API dev server restarting on a backend edit (tsx watch), reconnected on its own. `/ui-ux` audit run: three fixes applied (caveat chip flex-aligned icon + `px-2 py-1` tap padding; `text-nowrap` narrowed to the contract label); two noted deviations kept on purpose because the approved mockup specifies them — the mobile view is a card list rather than the full table (same pattern as Trade Alerts), and the long headers "Best opportunity" / "Not accounted for" / "Forecast RV".
- **SIGNALS SCREEN stage 4 of 5 BUILT + VERIFIED 2026-09-22 (the Signals modal; committed 2026-09-23, api `532bc8e` / app `b70efbd`).** Plain-language: clicking a ticker on the Signals screen opens a modal with the same header, Positions and Wheel-cycle cards as Ticker Detail, a live graded list of every candidate contract (filters: All / Puts / Calls, expiry buckets, Show Avoid), the selected expiry's option chain (calls left, puts right, only the out-of-the-money side is scored), a placeholder where order setup will go (stage 5), and the price / IV-history charts at the bottom. Kept in the URL as `?signal=SYMBOL`. **Decisions (Marcelo):** (1) header data via a new `sections` parameter on the existing ticker-detail stream (`/tickers/:symbol/detail/stream?sections=overview,chart,technicals`; default = everything, so Ticker Detail is unchanged; verified live: 13 pricing updates + chart + technicals, no spot line, no option chain; unknown section rejected) — 1 IBKR line for the modal header instead of ~98; (2) the Positions card wiring extracted into a shared `useTickerPositions` hook + `TickerPositionsCards`, and the header strip into `TickerHeaderStrip`, both used by Ticker Detail AND the Signals modal (one implementation; Ticker Detail re-tested with Playwright afterwards: header, Wheel cycle, alerts, chain with spot marker, technicals, chart, IV history all intact); (3) line budget: the same-tab "pause Signals while Ticker Detail is open" guard turned out to be moot — the Signals page never opens Ticker Detail and leaving the page closes its stream — so nothing was built; cross-tab overlap (Ticker Detail ~98 lines in one tab + Signals in another) REMAINS POSSIBLE and is only fixable by a central per-login line counter on the server (separate task, see open item 3 above); (4) the order-setup slot shows a placeholder card ("arrives in stage 5") with the selected candidate's summary. **Line cost of the modal:** 1 (header pricing) + 1 (Signals spot) + ≤ 40 option lines. **Verified with Playwright against the live dev servers (market open, 10:08 ET):** header strip live ($109.15, day range, volume, sector), signal row (ATM IV 88.6%, forecast 124.6%, IV − forecast −36 vp, momentum, skew, vol flag, shares/cash free, caveat chip), Wheel cycle card with AAOI's real cycle, opportunities list (313 candidates, all Avoid and hidden by default; "Show Avoid" reveals them ranked by Edge $), chain grid with spot marker, row click switches the chain's expiry and re-subscribes the live quote set (Oct 2 → 60 strikes), ESC/backdrop close clears the URL, light + dark, 1440 px + 390 px (no overflow). **Open item 1 CLOSED here:** with the market open, 36 of the 40 subscribed contracts carried live two-sided quotes (`quoteSource: "live"`, spread shown without the snapshot asterisk), 4 had a missing side and kept the 10:00 quote; UncompensatedShare filled for all 313 within the first refresh. Also added: `formatQuotePrice` (two-decimal quotes, no symbol) in the shared formatters, a `dense` option on `DataTable`, and the caveat chip moved to `components/signals/NotAccountedForChip.tsx` for both screens. Roll from the Positions card is not supported inside the Signals modal (a notice points to Ticker Detail); "Sell Call" from the card selects the matching scored candidate when there is one.
- **SIGNALS SCREEN stage 5 of 5 BUILT + VERIFIED 2026-09-22 (order setup; committed 2026-09-23, api `532bc8e` / app `b70efbd`). THE SIGNALS BUILD IS COMPLETE END-TO-END.** Plain-language: selecting a contract in the Signals modal now opens the order setup on the right: a Signal card that shows the net Edge you can expect at each fill priority (best case at the mid, worst case at the bid, the list's own score), a meter that warns if the score has moved since you picked the contract, the contract's live-vs-surface IV, delta-drift risk, tilt and flags spelled out, contracts and fill priority, and "Review Order", which goes through the SAME review-and-confirm panel every other order uses — nothing new touches IBKR. The scores at that moment are saved with the order so Phase 2 can test what predicted the result. **Decisions (Marcelo):** priority (Patient / Normal / Urgent) chosen in the setup form and carried into the review panel as its default (still changeable there); snapshot = the candidate as scored at build (IV, forecast, edge, friction, net Edge at bid AND mid, Edge $, vega, delta, spread, uncompensated share, flags, grade, quote source) + ticker context (spot, ATM IV, forecast, momentum, skew, vol flag, earnings, grade counts) + order (quantity, priority, reference premium, expected net Edge / Edge $) + timing (selected at, built at, net Edge at selection and at build); decay warning at ≥ 1 vp; a candidate you cannot place (no shares / no cash) shows the reason with Review Order disabled. **Expected fill model (mine, stated on screen):** an Adaptive order concedes 0% (Patient), 50% (Normal) or 100% (Urgent) of the way from the mid to the bid — so expected net Edge sits between `netEdgeAtMid` (commission only) and `netEdge` (half-spread + commission, the list's score). **Built:** backend — `SignalCandidate.vega / netEdgeAtMid / edgeDollarsAtMid` (tested against the commission-only friction), migration `20260922000002` adding nullable jsonb `order_requests.signal_snapshot` (applied locally, batch 34; kept OUT of `payload`, which the worker parses), `POST /positions/orders` accepts `signalSnapshot` (object only, ≤ 16 KB, stored as-is), the roadmap's friction item now counts real filled Signals orders (`signal_snapshot` set and status filled / partially_filled). Frontend — `SignalOrderSetupForm` (same form/review split as `RollOrderSetupForm`; builds via the existing `buildOpenOrder`), `OrderReviewPanel` gained `initialAdaptivePriority`, the modal's right pane swaps placeholder → form → shared review panel and reloads positions on a fill. Suite 434. **Verified:** (a) route: built an AAOI $90 put order through the real endpoint with a snapshot → row `pending_confirmation` with the jsonb stored; array rejected with 400; cancelled → `cancelled`, `ibkr_order_id` null. (b) Browser (market open): selected Put $90 Sep 25 → Signal card arithmetic matched the list (edge −16.6 = 108.0 − 124.6; friction 0.6 – 4.9 vp; best −17.2, worst −21.5 = the row's score; Normal = midpoint), decay meter steady, flags X and W spelled out, Review Order → the shared review panel opened with Normal preselected and its own live quote → Cancel → pane reset, DB row `cancelled` with the full snapshot (candidate + ticker + order + timing). No order was ever submitted to IBKR. **Market-hours observation 2026-09-22 12:31 ET (live quotes re-scoring):** with the $90 put selected, three of six live-quoted rows re-scored within 43 s (friction ±0.1 vp, net Edge ±0.1 vp) as spot moved; no bid/ask itself ticked in that window (thin $0.05-wide contracts), so a quote-DRIVEN flip is covered by the unit tests, not by a live observation. Decay meter read "steady" correctly. **A real fill through the Signals path is deliberately NOT tested locally:** the only gateway worker runs on the VPS against the production DB, so a paper order from a local DB would be reconciled INTO production as a position nobody created; do it on staging after the deploy instead. **Not done / follow-ups:** `/ui-ux` pass on the form (self-checked: semantic buttons, no `btn-sm` in the toolbar-level priority group, no nowrap on text); **Committed 2026-09-23** (api `532bc8e`, app `b70efbd`), not yet pushed. Push (staging auto-deploys) + the migrations on staging + a manual `--force` capture run + a paper-fill test are the next steps, held for Marcelo's go-ahead.

- **Risk-adjusted ratio (Edge $ / dollar risk) — approved, built as a computed-but-not-displayed field, 2026-09-23 (uncommitted).** Formula approved: `dollarRisk = strike*100 - premium` (CSP) or `spotPrice*100 - premium` (CC), at the mid; `riskAdjustedRatio = edgeDollars / dollarRisk` (and a `riskAdjustedRatioAtMid` sibling for `edgeDollarsAtMid`). Built into `SignalCandidate` (`signalCandidates.ts`, tested) and the app's mirrored type (`api/signals.ts`); the order-setup form derives an `expectedPremium` (same mid→bid concession interpolation as `netEdgeExpected`) so `riskAdjustedRatioExpected` is recorded in the order's `signalSnapshot.order` payload. **Decided NOT to surface it in the UI (Signals table, ticker modal, order-setup card) after discussion 2026-09-23:** Marcelo's stated goal is automating candidate selection quantitatively, so a column with no link to grading/ranking/sizing is qualitative noise, not a step toward that. It doesn't need new storage either — like Net Edge/Edge $, it is cheaply re-derivable at any historical point from what Phase 0 already persists (captured strike/quote/spot), so Phase 2 backtesting gets it for free by replaying `buildSignalCandidates` over history; no separate table needed. **Two real open questions surfaced, not yet decided:** (1) whether it becomes a 5th term in Composite Rank (Formula 6, currently `z(Edge) + w_dir·Tilt − w_unc·UncompensatedShare − w_fric·FrictionCost`) once Phase 2 backtest data can justify a weight, or (2) whether capital-at-risk normalization belongs at the Kelly/CVaR sizing layer (Formulas 1/5, Phase 3) instead of candidate ranking, to avoid risk-adjusting twice. Composite Rank's weights are explicitly "TBD in Phase 2" — none of this is answerable, including whether risk-adjusted ratio has any place in it, until backtest infrastructure exists. **Next real step toward automation is Phase 2 (backtest/calibrate), not another ranking metric.**
- **Dividend-schedule roadmap item DECIDED and BUILT, 2026-09-23 (uncommitted).** This closes the "Only the next dividend is in the forward / needs a dividend schedule source (none chosen)" item that sat as `waiting_on_decision` in the Signals roadmap and per-ticker caveats since the surface-fit build (2026-09-22). **Options presented:** (a) project a regular cadence from data already captured, no new vendor; (b) IBKR `reqFundamentalData`; (c) a third-party dividend API (Polygon.io etc.); (d) leave it open. **Chosen: (a).** **Formula approved and built** (`projectDividendSchedule` + `isRegularDividendCadence` in `impliedVolatilitySurface.ts`): given the next ex-dividend (already captured) and the most recent past one (already sitting in `ticker_calendar_events` from TradingView's "recent" column, just not previously queried for this), infer the cadence as the day-gap between them; if it falls in `[20, 400]` days (rejects glitches/specials, accepts monthly/quarterly/semiannual payers), project further ex-dividends at the SAME amount (no growth model) every `cadenceDays` until the furthest expiry being fit that day, capped at 6 projections as a safety ceiling. Falls back to today's single-next-dividend behavior, unchanged, whenever there's no past record or the gap looks irregular — no regression for any ticker. `computeForwardPrice` needed no change (it already summed/filtered multiple dividends correctly). Wired in `optionSurfaceStore.ts`'s `loadHeaders` (new `LEFT JOIN LATERAL` on `ticker_calendar_events` for the past ex-div row, mirroring the existing lateral-join pattern in `pricePerformanceSnapshot.ts`) and `optionSurfaceFitting.ts` (`SurfaceSnapshotInput` gained `pastExDividendDate/Amount`). **Roadmap updated to match:** the global "dividends" item removed from `buildSignalsRoadmap` (decided and built, not open anymore); the per-ticker caveat (`signalsRoadmap.ts` `buildTickerCaveats`) renamed from `hasDividendEvents` (any ex-div ever, even for a ticker that stopped paying — always true for payers, regardless of whether the forward could account for it) to `dividendCadenceUnknown` (true only when a next ex-div exists but no cadence could be projected), computed in `signalsStore.ts`'s new `loadDividendCadenceUnknown`; status changed `waiting_on_decision` → `waiting_on_data` (needs one more observed cycle for that specific ticker, not a decision anymore) and the copy corrected to describe the actual remaining gap. Both API and app repos' `hasDividendEvents` type field renamed to match (`api/signals.ts` unused elsewhere in the app). Tests: `impliedVolatilitySurface.test.ts` (+11), `optionSurfaceFitting.test.ts` (+1 covering both the regular-cadence projection and the irregular-gap fallback against `computeForwardPrice` directly), `signalsRoadmap.test.ts` updated. Suite 444 → 446, `tsc` clean in both repos.

- **Tilt APPROVED and BUILT 2026-09-22 (uncommitted; nothing consumes it yet).** Marcelo approved three separate, unblended display measures (no weights) and asked for the flag threshold to be each ticker's own 90th percentile, after more data was fetched to test it. Data: 11 more shortlist tickers backfilled in dev (17 of 18 now have 5 years; DRAM only 118 days). Flag comparison on 15 tickers, 12.8k observations, expanding-window (no lookahead): own-p90 rule gap 0.10 (flagged next-21d vol 1.11× trailing YZ126 vs 1.01×; 53% vs 41% of flagged windows stay above the trailing level), fixed 1.3 gap 0.06 (flags 6–20% of days depending on ticker), fixed 1.5 gap 0.01 (4% of days). The effect is modest (the six-name +17-point difference I first saw did not survive normalising by each ticker's own level). `src/lib/tiltMeasures.ts` (momentum 12-1, skew from the slice nearest 30 days, flag with own-p90 and a fixed-1.3 fallback until 250 earlier observations) and `tiltStore.ts` (bars up to and including the snapshot date). 13 tests, 15 mutations: 13 caught, 1 equivalent (the 252-vs-253 guard is backed by a later null check), 1 tautology in my own test fixed and re-caught. Real check (21 Sep): momentum +175% MU … −102% BMNR (its history is odd, see below), nobody elevated today (all ratios 0.6–1.1 vs thresholds 1.15–1.52), only AAOI has skew (−2.4 vp @32d: calls richer than puts). Artifact Formula 3f + decision. **Bug found while backfilling:** IBKR returned a fractional volume ('134.5') for BMNR and DELL and the bigint insert failed the whole 5Y history step; fixed with `normalizeBarVolume` (3 tests) in `priceBarCache.ts` (daily and intraday writes) and the nightly `run-daily-market-data-job.ts`, which had the same latent bug. BMNR's history shows several suspected splits and only 293 IV points: exclude it from any statistics.
- **Tilt exploration, 2026-09-22 (tmp/tiltSpike.ts; formula NOT yet approved).** Momentum 12−1 (ln P[t−21]/P[t−252]) vs the next 21-day return on AAOI, SMCI, TLT, HOOD, PLTR, COIN: Spearman −0.20…+0.09 per ticker, pooled +0.022 with a ±0.12 noise band (about 280 effective observations), so nothing can be confirmed or refuted on six names; it needs hundreds of stocks. Skew cannot be tested at all yet (one day of chains). High-volatility regime, YZ21/YZ126: p50 0.94, p75 1.10, **p90 1.29**, p95 1.41; when the ratio is in the top decile the NEXT 21 days realize 85% vol vs 68% otherwise (vol clustering, the one effect that shows up clearly), forward returns are not distinguishable (5.8% vs 4.1%, noisy). (The last statistic my script printed was a bug and is ignored.)
- **UncompensatedShare APPROVED and BUILT 2026-09-22 (uncommitted; nothing consumes it yet).** Marcelo approved the simulation (Israelov & Nielsen decomposition) at the SVI-fitted implied volatility at the strike; a separate display column, never blended. `src/lib/uncompensatedShare.ts`: 4,000 antithetic GBM paths, seeded, one step per trading day, zero drift/rate (my implementation details, disclosed), returns timing/equity/volatility shares (sum to exactly 1) and the entry delta; null for bad inputs or a deep-ITM call with no variance to apportion. 14 tests incl. four cases against an independent pure-Python simulation (±0.03 band, MC noise ~0.01) and scale invariance; 12 mutations, 9 caught, 3 survivors judged equivalent (dt = T/steps vs 1/252 when T is a whole number of trading days; the antithetic sign; the at-expiry delta, which is never used). ~10 ms per candidate. Real check (AAOI 23 Oct, IV ≈ 87%): call-equivalent strikes 26% timing at the money → 18% at 8% OTM; a put 17% OTM shows 50%. **Caveat to carry into the UI:** it is a share, not an amount — a far-OTM put has a big timing share of a small total risk; show the absolute dollar risk next to it (not built, not yet decided). Artifact Formula 3e + decision (v10).
- **UncompensatedShare simulation, 2026-09-22 (tmp/timingShareSpike.ts; formula NOT yet approved).** Israelov & Nielsen (2015) decomposition of a covered call's P&L variance by simulation (GBM, daily steps, 3–6k paths): equity = (1−Δ₀)·dS, TIMING = −(Δ_{t−1} − Δ₀)·dS (delta drift: the uncompensated bet), vol = −(dC − Δ_{t−1}dS) (gamma/theta); shares = Cov(component, total)/Var(total). **Sanity check against the paper passes:** ATM monthly, σ 16% gives equity 73% / timing 25% (paper: ~67% / ~25%; our vol share 2% vs their 8% because σ is constant here). At σ 60%: timing share ≈ 22–27% ATM at any expiry, 13–22% at 5% OTM, 7–18% at 10% OTM, 2–10% at 20% OTM, rising with days to expiry. Cash-secured puts are the same by put-call parity (short put + cash ≈ covered call), so one function covers both.
- **Friction cost APPROVED and BUILT 2026-09-22 (uncommitted; nothing consumes it yet).** NetEdge = Edge − [λ·(ask−bid)/2 + c/100] / Vega, λ = 1.0 (Marcelo chose the hit-the-bid setting over my 0.5 recommendation) and c = $0.68/contract (mean of the 94 stored option trades). `src/lib/optionFriction.ts` (`blackScholesVega`, `computeFrictionCost`, `computeNetEdge`; 10 tests with independent Python reference values incl. a finite-difference vega check; 12 mutations all caught). No two-sided quote → no estimate, no net score. Real-data check (AAOI 23 Oct puts, 21 Sep snapshot): Edge is ≈ −37…−38 vp on every strike, friction 3–8 vp, so the strike-to-strike differences in net Edge come almost entirely from the quoted spread (noisy at the open). Artifact Formula 3d + decision. Remaining formulas: Tilt, UncompensatedShare (both shown as separate columns, not blended).
- **Friction-cost analysis, 2026-09-22 (tmp/frictionSpike.ts; formula NOT yet approved).** Real AAOI chain (10:10 ET snapshot, 333 OTM contracts with two-sided quotes): the half-spread expressed in vol points (half-spread ÷ vega × 100, the Edge given up by selling at the bid instead of the mid) is ~5 vp in the typical alert region (|Δ| 0.15–0.30, 20–60 d; median 4.66), 1–3 vp beyond 45 d, 6–8 vp under 14 d and for |Δ| < 0.1 (relative spreads 17–67%). Your own stored trades show a mean option commission of $0.68/contract (94 trades), worth only ~0.07 vp: the spread is the whole story. Caveats: one wide-spread ticker at the busy open; alerts will use live quotes at their own time.
- **Volatility forecast + Edge APPROVED and BUILT 2026-09-22 (uncommitted; nothing consumes it yet).** Marcelo approved: RV_forecast = trailing 63-day Yang-Zhang for every expiry; fallback to the 21-day window, else no Edge (alert shown unscored); expiries spanning an earnings date are flagged, not adjusted. `src/lib/volatilityEdge.ts` (forecast selection, `computeVolatilityEdge` = √(w(k)/T) − forecast, unscored for any non-'ok' slice, extrapolation marker, `expirySpansEarnings`; 9 tests, 12 mutations all caught), `volatilityForecastStore.ts` (bars up to and including the snapshot date only, so no lookahead). Artifact Formula 3c + two decisions (v7). Real-data check (AAOI snapshot 2026-09-21): forecast 124.6% (YZ63) vs ATM IV 88–100% → Edge −24 to −36 vol points on every expiry, 60d/88d flagged SPANS EARNINGS (11-05). **CORRECTION 2026-09-22 (my earlier explanation was wrong):** I first attributed AAOI's negative Edge to the 08-06 earnings gap; the breakdown shows AAOI's 63-day window has many overnight gaps of 8–15% (08-04 +15.5%, 08-07 +13.7%, 08-24 −13.2%, 07-31 +10.5%) and removing the earnings day only moves YZ63 from 124.6% to 121.2%. The negative Edge is real (recent realized ~120% vs IV 88–100%, IV rank 7%), not an artefact. **Ex-earnings variant: not adopted, not validatable yet.** A price/volume gap-detection proxy (tmp/exEarningsBacktest.ts) suggests dropping event days lowers the forecast bias in event-free windows (0.148 → 0.049) but raises it in event windows (−0.17 → −0.28); it is biased because it also removes unscheduled jumps, and TradingView's scanner returns only the LAST and NEXT earnings dates (no history), so scheduled earnings cannot be back-tested. Revisit once the daily calendar capture has accumulated a few quarters of dates (each night stores the last-reported date), or if a history source is bought. The approved flag-not-adjust rule stands. Only AAOI has a snapshot in dev, so no cross-ticker view yet.
- **Volatility-forecast back-test, 2026-09-22 (tmp/volForecastBacktest.ts, exploratory; formula NOT yet approved).** Data: 5Y daily bars + IV for AAOI, SMCI, TLT, HOOD, PLTR, COIN (backfilled in dev through the pipeline, sequentially, all fine after a transient ECONNRESET on TLT's first attempt; retried OK). Task: forecast the NEXT h trading days of Yang-Zhang realized vol from data up to t, h = 10, 21, 42, 63 (overlapping windows, so effective n ≈ 300 per horizon; one 2021-26 regime). Loss QLIKE (Patton 2011; lower is better). **Trailing YZ63 is best or within noise of best at every horizon** (h=21: YZ63 0.155 vs YZ21 0.222, YZ10 0.267, close-to-close 21d 0.288, EWMA94 0.209; h=63: YZ63 0.116 vs IV itself 0.131); matching the window to the option's expiry did not help, short windows are clearly worse; blends (YZ21+YZ63, YZ21+YZ126, HAR-style) are within noise of YZ63. **IV as a forecaster** has the lowest QLIKE at h ≤ 21 (0.116 at 21). **No average volatility premium on these names:** mean ln(IV/realized) = +0.015, −0.015, −0.035, −0.043 at h = 10/21/42/63 and IV exceeds realized only 43–57% of the time (consistent with the literature that single-stock premia are small vs. the index). So Edge = IV − forecast is a cross-sectional ranking signal, not an average-positive number, and its predictive link to the realized premium is modest (correlation 0.05–0.13 at h=21, 0.2–0.3 at h=42/63). Not tested: earnings adjustment, the index/ETF-only book, other regimes.
- **DECIDED 2026-09-22 (Marcelo): composite rank.** Retention: keep the chain archive indefinitely (~218 B/row, ~260 MB/yr at ~4.7k rows/night; Essential-1 is 10 GB), and do NOT exclude the chain tables from sync-dev-db. Phase 1 ranks alerts by NET EDGE only (surface IV minus volatility forecast, less friction); Tilt and UncompensatedShare are shown as separate columns and no blend weights are asserted until Phase 2 can fit them on real outcomes (Formula 6 stays placeholder-weighted; only ~1 day of chains exists today). Formula sign-off order: (1) volatility forecast for Edge, (2) friction cost, (3) Tilt, (4) UncompensatedShare. Volatility forecast is next.
- **Nightly surface-fit job BUILT 2026-09-22 (Marcelo: 'go'; uncommitted; not yet run on a live capture).** Table `option_surface_fits` (migration 20260922000001, batch 32 local): one row per (snapshot, expiry) with the raw-SVI a,b,ρ,m,σ, status, point count, dropped-quote counts, RMSE, min butterfly density, k range and the calendar check against the previous fitted expiry; derived data, replaced wholesale on a re-fit, cascades with the snapshot. `src/lib/optionSurfaceFitting.ts` (pure, 8 tests, 8 mutations caught), `optionSurfaceStore.ts` (DB), `runOptionSurfaceFitJob.ts`, `npm run job:option-surface-fit [-- --date YYYY-MM-DD] [SYMBOL…]`, and the chained capture script now runs capture → surface fit → alerts (a fit failure never blocks alerts). Design choices of mine (reversible): one table, no second per-snapshot table; a snapshot with no risk-free rate is skipped and reported, not fitted with r=0; 0DTE expiries (T=0) are not stored; only complete/partial snapshots are fitted. Verified on the real AAOI chain loaded into the dev tables: 8/8 expiries ok, identical to the library run, re-run replaces (no duplicates), job_runs row written, bad/empty dates handled. Watchdog not required for this job (it is chained; a failure is in job_runs and Telegram via runJob).
- **SVI surface library BUILT 2026-09-22 (uncommitted; not yet wired to any job or screen).** `src/lib/impliedVolatilitySurface.ts`: forward, Black-Scholes-on-forward + IV inversion, OTM-only quote filter (bid>0, ask>bid, spread ≤50%), raw SVI fit (quasi-explicit inner LS + refined grid over (m,σ), projected onto b≥0, |ρ|≤0.98, σ≥0.02, positivity), butterfly density and calendar checks, slice gate (≥8 points, RMSE ≤3 vol pts, flags not silent use). 31 offline tests with independent Python reference values (finite-difference g(k)); 18 hand-made mutations all caught (5 initially survived and drove 4 extra tests). Run on the real AAOI chain it reproduces the exploratory fit: all 8 slices ok, RMSE 0.33–1.91 vol pts, min g > 0.71, 0 calendar violations; ρ hits 0.98 on 2 slices and σ hits 0.02 on 4 (the watch item). Artifact updated (Formula 3b + decision). Known gaps: only the next ex-dividend is stored (later dividends before a 60–90 d expiry are missing from the forward); early exercise ignored; nothing consumes the surface yet (Phase 1 Edge score, PoP).
- **SVI exploratory fit on the real AAOI chain, 2026-09-22 (tmp/sviSpike.ts, not app code; formulas NOT yet approved).** Raw SVI per expiry, quasi-explicit (Zeliade) calibration on OTM mids (bid>0, spread ≤50% of mid), forward from spot and the risk-free rate. 8 expiries: RMSE 0.3–1.9 vol points, butterfly g(k) > 0 everywhere (min 0.71), 0 calendar violations in 147 checks; SVI ATM IV runs 1–3 vol points above IBKR's tick-13 near-ATM IV. BUT per-expiry parameters are unstable (ρ at ±1 on 3 of 8 slices, σ at the search floor on 4) and the 60d/88d monthly expiries have only 16–22 usable points: good curves, uninterpretable/erratic parameters, so wings and tail probabilities are the risk. Recent literature checked (search results only, papers not read in full): Corbetta et al. 2019 (robust SSVI slices), Martini & Mingone (No-arbitrage SVI, SIAM J. Fin. Math. 2022), Ferhati 2020 (arbitrage-free SVI calibration), Hendriks & Martini 2019 / 2022 global eSSVI parametrization.
- **DECIDED 2026-09-22 (Marcelo): existing tickers are backfilled from the UI, one at a time, not by a timed script.** The 5-year script's fixed pacing (my untested 10 s guess, above IBKR's ~60 history requests/10 min at 18 tickers) is dropped as the route for staging/prod. Built: `GET /shortlist` rows carry `historyIncomplete` (under ~5 years of bars AND no pipeline run has ever completed the history step, so an IPO'd ticker clears after one successful run) and `historyStartDate`; the shortlist Status column shows an amber 'Backfill history' button that starts the full pipeline and opens the progress modal; every such button is disabled while any ticker is preparing (one at a time, paced by the user). Playwright-verified on the dev DB (15 flagged, TLT run end to end, its flag cleared, the other 14 re-enabled, no horizontal scroll at 390 px). `npm run backfill:signal-engine-history` stays as a local/dev tool only (its pacing default is untested). No bulk 'backfill all' button (not requested).
- **Pipeline orchestration tests 2026-09-22 (uncommitted):** `tickerBackfillPipeline.ts` now takes an injectable run store and step workers (defaults unchanged); `executeBackfillRun` and `waitForBackfillQueue` exported. `tickerBackfillPipeline.test.ts` (16 tests, offline, in-memory store): all four steps on one connection and one disconnect; market closed → snapshot skipped; market open → snapshot with Eastern date and rate as percent (null passes through); a failing step is marked failed with its error while later steps still run (run ends partial); unresolved calendar = skipped, run stays complete; strike-step failure → snapshot skipped with the reason; IBKR unreachable → IBKR steps fail, calendar still runs; split note in the history message; progress monotonic to 100%; store failure still disconnects and finishes; start returns at once with a running run; joins an in-progress run; fresh run after a finished one; closes a leftover running row before creating; tickers run strictly one at a time; a crashed run does not block the queue. 11 hand-made mutations all caught. Message fix: the split note no longer says bars are 'unadjusted'. Regression run against real IBKR after the refactor: AAOI 23 s, all steps as expected (a first attempt hit a transient ECONNRESET and correctly ended 'partial'; the retry succeeded). Suite: 222 passing, tsc clean.
- **LIVE TEST AT THE OPEN, 2026-09-21 10:10 ET (paper Gateway, real-time data).** (1) Probe AMAT 9 contracts: real bid/ask/sizes/volume/OI arrive, market-data type real_time, no delayed ticks. (2) Real orchestrator on AAOI (single-ticker universe, dev DB): 339 contracts, 6 batches, 46 s capture / 63 s total, status complete, 339/339 with ticks and two-sided quotes, 282 (83%) with IBKR IV+delta, 263 (78%) with open interest, no crossed quotes, call/put delta signs all correct, header (spot 106.14, rate 3.72%, ref IV 0.84, real_time) saved and read back; process exited by itself (shutdown fix works). **Findings:** IBKR's tick-13 IV is nearly flat across strikes while IV from mids shows a skew, and ~17% of contracts never get tick 13 (not a timing issue): the archive keeps raw bid/ask so IV is recomputed from mids; expect ~80% IBKR-IV coverage. (3) Shared-connection retest (checklist below): 3 Ticker Detail modals in 3 isolated browser contexts each loaded the right ticker and kept updating, and closing one did not stop the others; 2 Dashboard tabs + Positions all updated live (16/16/15 changed numbers in 12 s), no stuck spinners after a Positions reload. Not isolated: the greeks stream on its own. No stall seen, so no revert needed.
- **Capture orchestrator tests 2026-09-21 (uncommitted):** `runOptionChainCapture` and `prepareTicker` now take injectable dependencies (defaults unchanged), `src/ibkr/runOptionChainCapture.test.ts` (17 tests, offline): 0–90 DTE bounds inclusive, expiry attached to contracts, validation groups ≤20, non-existent strikes dropped, reference-volatility vs widest window, missing contract id / spot, ticker failure recorded and run continues, statuses tallied from final result, starved re-capture once (45-min job budget, 10-min pass budget, failed re-capture keeps first result), disconnect always. 10 hand-made mutations all caught. Suite: 206 passing, tsc clean.
- **Capture job + capture→alerts orchestrator BUILT 2026-09-21, not yet run against IBKR (uncommitted).** Plain language: the nightly job now exists in code; it has been type-checked and unit-tested but never run against the real Gateway. Files: `src/ibkr/runOptionChainCapture.ts` (universe = shortlist + open-position tickers, no hardcoding; layered no-IV fallback; 0–90 DTE; strike validation capped at 20 in flight; one ticker at a time in batches of 60; one time-boxed re-capture of starved tickers; failed tickers get a `failed` header row), `src/lib/optionChainCaptureReferenceVolatility.ts` (the fallback rule, tested), `calendarDaysUntilExpiry` in `optionChainCaptureWindow.ts` (tested), `src/ibkr/runScheduledTradeAlertJob.ts` (the alert job body, extracted so both scripts share it), `scripts/run-option-chain-capture-job.ts` + `npm run job:option-chain-capture [-- --force]` (clock guard 10:00–10:30 ET, capture then alerts; alerts always run). Decisions (Marcelo, 2026-09-21): cap 20, shortlist + held, sequential per ticker. **Decided by me, reversible, needs his OK**: `--force` flag to bypass the clock guard for manual runs; spot price via the existing `fetchLivePrices` (may fall back to a stored last-known price if no live last); the strike window is not sized from a stale IV. Still to do: run it once against the paper Gateway at market open (ask first), watchdog `DAILY_JOB_NAMES` entry, Scheduler entries (Marcelo, manual), 5-year backfill.
- **Decided**: Yang-Zhang formula approved as written (10/21/63/126-day windows, ×252 annualization, earnings gaps kept in; pure library + tests, no endpoint yet). Split guard = the ±70% rule **plus** a split-ratio detector (moves near ½, ⅓, ¼, 1/5, 1/10 and reverse splits) **plus** a volume-jump confirmation — added because ±70% alone misses 2:1 and 3:1 splits. Chain archive includes **0DTE** (DTE 0–90) and captures **SPY, QQQ, IWM** as extra context tickers beyond shortlist + held (data only, no change to what's traded; single stocks only have 0DTE on their weekly-expiry Fridays). Archive stores calls and puts on their OTM side only (plus the at-the-money pair).
- **Measured**: 191 bytes per chain-archive contract row (incl. PK index, on a scratch temp table), 198 bytes per daily-bar row, prod DB 29 MB today. 5-year daily-bar backfill ≈ 5 MB. Chain archive ≤ ~11k contracts/night ≈ ≤ ~540 MB/year (upper bound; weeklies list fewer strikes than the strike-union used). A volatility-scaled strike window (±2σ√T, clamped 5–50%) costs the same ~11.1k contracts as a fixed ±25% (11.3k) but covers short-DTE and long-DTE/high-IV names properly.
- **Also decided 2026-09-21**: capture at **10:00 ET** daily; the alert scan is **chained after the capture completes** (one orchestrator script: capture → alerts, replacing the separate 13:30 UTC alert entry — Marcelo's proposal); **no separate liquidity test** (every archived contract carries bid/ask/size/OI, so the archive itself shows quote quality; thin quotes are stored and filtered at model-read time, not dropped at capture); strike window = **±2σ·√(DTE/365) around spot, clamped to 5–50%**, OTM side only + at-the-money pair (≈11.1k contracts/night at 0–90 DTE, ≈7.7k at 0–45 for the 19 shortlist/held tickers); extra capture tickers narrowed to **SPY and QQQ** (IWM dropped for now — SPY/QQQ list daily expiries at $1 strikes, unmeasured estimate +5–8k contracts/night for all three); DST handled with **two Scheduler entries (14:00 and 15:00 UTC) + a clock-time guard** so exactly one runs per day; **5-year daily-bar backfill approved** (~5.5 MB total, ~4.4 MB net new; still ask before running it against the paper Gateway).
- **Expiry cap decided 2026-09-21: 0–90 DTE** (Marcelo initially proposed 0–45, then chose 90 after seeing the maturity/rolling literature below; current `strategy_settings` are 1–14 DTE, delta 0.20–0.40 for both strategies). ≈11.1k contracts/night for the 19 shortlist/held tickers (upper bound) plus an unmeasured ~5–8k for SPY+QQQ.

The artifact is set to "Anyone with the link" (public share URL https://claude.ai/artifact/EJQFe3Fpfs8Zi3R27dZVb8 — viewers of a share link may be pinned to an earlier version); the owner link above stays canonical. Fixed two wrong cross-references in the original (§09→§10, §08→§09).
- **Chain-archive design spec approved 2026-09-21** (all four recommended options): (1) two tables as written — `option_chain_snapshots` (header per ticker per capture) + `option_quote_snapshots` (per contract; bid/ask/last/sizes, IBKR model IV + greeks, model price, spot used, open interest, volume); if IBKR won't deliver one of the new fields (sizes/OI/volume) in streaming mode, adjust after the first live test; (2) a same-day re-run **replaces** that day's snapshot; (3) a failed or partial capture **never blocks** the chained alert scan (failure still alerts via the runJob wrapper); (4) quota contention handled by **smaller batches (~60 lines) + re-capturing starved tickers** at the end of the run.
- **OPEN DECISIONS — Signal Engine Phase 0 (consolidated 2026-09-21; all need Marcelo's call, nothing below is assumed)**:
  1. **What to build next** (updated 2026-09-21): the capture module's logic is now built offline; what remains is the job + capture→alerts orchestrator (blocked on items 2 and 3 below), the watchdog entry (deliberately held until the Scheduler entries exist, else it would raise a "has not run today" alert every day), and the 5-year backfill script (no order chosen yet). Also: whether to merge the new collector into `fetchQuotesForContracts` instead of keeping it separate.
  2. ~~Ticker with no usable daily IV~~ **DECIDED 2026-09-21: layered fallback** — latest IV if ≤ ~5 days old → 21-day Yang-Zhang realized volatility → widest ±50% window. (Context, checked: today all 19 tickers have a current daily IV, thinnest is SPCX with 18 points — so this only arises for a brand-new ticker before its history backfill lands, or an IBKR IV-series gap). Options laid out for Marcelo: (a) layered fallback — latest IV if ≤ ~5 days old → 21-day Yang-Zhang realized vol → widest ±50% window (recommended: never skips, uses tested data before guessing); (b) skip the ticker and record a `failed` snapshot (no guessed window, but that night's data is lost); (c) always the widest ±50% window; (d) probe the live at-the-money contracts first and take their IV (most accurate, more code and live-data dependency). Chosen: (a).
  (Also settled here, no decision needed: `computeStrikeWindow` deliberately still returns null rather than inventing a fallback; the job applies whichever policy Marcelo picks.)

- **Code facts the spec rests on (2026-09-21)**: `fetchQuotesForContracts` (`src/ibkr/fetchOptionChain.ts`) requests no generic ticks and ignores sizes, open interest, volume, model price and `undPrice` (the last two arrive free on `tickOptionComputation`); it uses streaming `reqMktData`, ≤~96 lines, an 8s ceiling, and a comment notes the market-data line quota is shared Gateway-wide (concurrent scans exhausted it 2026-08-31). Strike validation via cached `reqContractDetails` (`option_chain_strike_checks`) means the first capture night validates thousands of new combinations. The watchdog's `DAILY_JOB_NAMES` (`src/lib/runWatchdogCheck.ts`) needs the new job added. Existing `trade_alert_generation` job script guards only on `isMarketClosedToday`.
- **Latent issue spotted 2026-09-21, not confirmed or fixed**: trade-alert generation is scheduled at a fixed 13:30 UTC (market open only during EDT) and its only guard is `isMarketClosedToday` (weekend/holiday), not time of day — from 2026-11-01 (EST) it would appear to fire at 8:30 ET, pre-market. Raised with Marcelo; relevant to any fixed-UTC capture schedule too.

**Phase 0 audit (2026-09-21) — what already exists vs. what's missing**:
- **Already in place, reusable as-is**: `daily_price_bars` full OHLCV + one blended IBKR `OPTION_IMPLIED_VOLATILITY` value/day (~1yr for 18 tickers, clean — zero null/invalid OHLC, zero missing-day gaps in the last year; unadjusted for splits, known limitation); `ticker_calendar_events` earnings + ex-dividend via TradingView (only ~30 days back / 90 ahead, history accrues forward only); `risk_free_rates` (TB3MS, latest value only, no history); `market_calendar`; `ivMetrics.ts` (IV Rank/Percentile); `technicalIndicators.ts`; `blackScholesPop.ts`. Everything Yang-Zhang needs (daily O/H/L/C, RTH-only, consistent) is already stored — that engine needs no new capture, just the formula.
- **The real gap — option chains are never persisted.** `fetchOptionChain.ts` streams live quotes for the Ticker Detail modal only, capped at IBKR's ~100 market-data lines (6 expiries × 4 strikes/side × 2 sides × 2 rights = 96) — far too sparse to fit an SVI smile (5 params/expiry) and thrown away after display. No per-strike IV/bid/ask/OI/volume history exists anywhere, and IBKR can't backfill it — so **every day without an archive is permanently lost calibration/backtest data**. This is the time-critical Phase 0 item.
- **Other gaps**: history depth is ~1yr (12-1 month momentum needs ≥~14 months; vol-regime context wants more); no market-context series (SPY/VIX) for the vol-regime dampener the directional tilt needs; several newer tickers (SNDK, GS, SPCX) have <75 bars/≤18 IV points.

- **Positions table: Price, P(Δ), P(d2), totals row, 2026-09-19 (both repos, uncommitted; dev-verified only, not deployed).** Removed Opened/Delta/Gamma columns (per-leg gamma stays in PositionCard inside Ticker Detail; IBKR gamma is **per $1** of underlying — verified against 21 stored snapshots: a per-1% reading implies impossible 300-850% IVs on MU/AMAT). Added **Price** (option tick's `undPrice`, else stock-leg market value ÷ shares), **P(Δ)** (short call |Δ|, short put 1−|Δ|) and **P(d2)** (N(d2), IV + underlying from the same `tickOptionComputation` tick, FRED TB3MS risk-free rate; threshold = strike, or max(strike, stock cost basis) for a CC), both 0–1 with 1 = success, computed server-side in the `/positions/greeks/stream` (`positionSuccessProbability.ts`, `computeSuccessProbability` in `blackScholesPop.ts`). Risk-free rate: `risk_free_rates` table (migration 20260919000001, run on dev only — **prod needs `migrate:latest:prod` and `FRED_API_KEY` in Heroku config**), refreshed from FRED after 20 days, last good value kept on FRED failure, "—" if never fetched. New **totals row** (`DataTable` `footerCells`, `computePositionTotals`): sums P&L $/Premium/Stock/Exp $/Exp %, P&L % = total P&L ÷ total Exp; Closed view shows P&L $ totals only. Verified in Playwright (dark/light, 1600px and 390px) and by hand-checking the totals against the row values.
  **Wheel-cycle engine + Cycle card + strategy scoreboard BUILT 2026-09-19 (committed, deploys with the next push; dev-verified):** `src/lib/cycles.ts` (+ `cycleQueries.ts`) derives every cycle per symbol at read time (boundaries from legs, cash ledger from trades fills + ITM-at-expiry assignments from daily bars) and attributes it to CSP / Unstructured / CC per the approved formulas (stock owned by CC while a call is open on it, else Unstructured; handoffs at the daily close of the transition date; put assignment charges the CSP bucket close-vs-strike; buy-write shares start at their cost). Routes `GET /positions/cycles?symbol=` and `GET /positions/cycles/scoreboard` (cycles with `dataFlags` are left out of the scoreboard and listed). UI: `CycleCard` in Ticker Detail (a collapsible 'Wheel cycle' card, not a tab — the modal has no tabs) and a collapsible `CycleScoreboard` above the Positions table. **Independent check:** closed-cycle totals equal the closed positions' realized P&L for all 13 symbols with closed cycles to within $0.40 (dev DB after the audit job + the HOOD fills patch); buckets always sum to the cycle total. As of last close / last nightly snapshot, not live. **Break-even is the PRO-RATA formula — Marcelo chose it over the whole-cycle cash version 2026-09-19** ((cost of all shares acquired - net premium) / shares acquired; AAOI 101.67). Capital in the scoreboard counts every handoff as a deployment, so Return is conservative; annualised/time-weighted return remains the low-priority follow-up. **Two data findings while validating:** (1) the audit job now also sets `exit_price = 0` on expired short option legs whose exit_price is NULL with no closing trade (MU's two 8/26 calls: $1,448.28 of premium missing from realized P&L) — the prod job is already in `apply` mode, so this extra correction runs on the first run after the push; (2) HOOD 8/24 partial fills missing from `trades` (call buy-back 3 not 1, stock sale 100+101+99) — `tmp/fix_hood_missing_fills.sql` (guarded, prod-run by Marcelo) completes them; until then HOOD's first cycle is flagged and excluded from the scoreboard.
**Dashboard P&L cards moved to the fair cycle attribution, 2026-09-21 (both repos, uncommitted; dev-verified against a fresh prod copy, not deployed).** "P&L by Period" and "P&L by Strategy (YTD)" now use the same CSP / N/S / CC buckets as the Positions scoreboard (`src/lib/cyclePeriodPnl.ts`, wired in `routes/dashboard.ts`; rows relabelled with `StrategyBadge`, order CC / CSP / N/S / Residual / Total). Formula (approved by Marcelo): period P&L(bucket) = bucket total now - bucket total *as of* the baseline day, where "as of" is the same `deriveCycles` run on the ledger truncated at the end of that Eastern day (later fills dropped, later-closed legs reopened, shares marked at that day's close, open options at that day's `position_pnl_snapshots.premium_pnl`); YTD unrealized = open marks now - open marks at baseline, realized = YTD - unrealized; Residual stays the plug against the account totals. Baselines: Day = trading day before the *latest account snapshot* (the account Day is that snapshot's daily P&L); WTD/MTD/YTD = trading day before Monday / the 1st / Jan 1. Price basis is daily (latest `daily_price_bars` close + latest snapshot mark), same as the old cards; opening a 1Y/5Y/All chart mid-session can also write today's partial bar. **Verified:** YTD bucket totals equal the scoreboard to the cent (CSP 21,831.63 / N/S -3,367.14 / CC 13,318.27); Day total ties to the account's 2026-09-18 daily P&L (7,653.66) with Residual -104. **MTD caveat:** `premium_pnl`/`stock_pnl` are NULL on every snapshot through 2026-09-08, so an option open at Aug 31 can only be baselined when its position is pure short puts (then unrealized = premium P&L; call-only positions can carry stock P&L from a reassigned stock leg: AAOI/AMAT/SPCX 2026-09-09..11 broke the naive "option-only" rule). Six cycles are left out of MTD and land in Residual: AAOI, AMAT, BMNR, HOOD (stock/calls), COIN, SMH (no Aug 31 snapshot). Hence MTD Residual (+11,345) > YTD Residual (+5,739). Removed `computeStrategyPeriodPnl` (unused); the P&L Over Time chart still uses the old per-position attribution (`computeStrategyDailyPnlSeries`). Scoreboard also lost its Capital/Return columns (API still returns them). Tests: `cyclePeriodPnl.test.ts`.

**YTD Residual (+$5,739) investigated, 2026-09-21 — not fully explained.** Account YTD is just NLV(09-18) - NLV(08-24) = 37,522 (no cash flows), so both cards only span since the platform's first snapshot (08-24), despite the "YTD" label. No cycles are excluded from YTD (Dec 31 baseline), so the gap is real. Replaying `deriveCycles` as of every snapshot day (tmp/residualDailyDiagnostic.ts) shows the cumulative gap wobbling between -5.2k (09-02) and +7.5k (09-08) with single days off by up to +/-5k: mostly mark/timing basis noise (see the 2026-09-11 capture-timing finding), with a positive drift of ~$500/day over 09-09..09-18. Interest/dividends are NOT the explanation: Flex shows none (paper account DUR854038; the Flex query is `LastNCalendarDays`, currently only 09-14..09-18, Cash Transactions empty). To itemise fully, Marce would need to change the Flex query in Client Portal (custom date range from 2026-08-24; add Trades with commission and Change in NAV). MTD Residual (+11,345) is inflated by six cycles dropped for having no Aug 31 option mark (see above). UI: Residual label on both cards is now a dotted-underline `DottedLabelTooltip` (components/HelpTooltip.tsx) with a footnote; verified desktop dark + mobile touch light, no overflow. The Dashboard "P&L Over Time" chart is now stacked columns (one bar per day, Residual on top; the stack total equals the account's daily P&L) instead of overlapping areas, because ApexCharts stacked areas cross over on mixed-sign data.
## Recent activity log (2026-09-08 → 2026-09-20)

**Pulse resizable side panels, 2026-09-22.** *In plain terms:* on the Pulse screen you can now drag the line between the two left panels (Positions / Top Alerts) and between the two right panels (Trades / Latest Events) to make one taller and the other shorter. Each side remembers its own setting in your browser; double-click the line to put it back to half-and-half. Desktop only. *Technical:* `components/pulse/ResizableRail.tsx` wraps each rail; the split is the top panel's share (%) in localStorage (`pulse.leftRailTopPercent` / `pulse.rightRailTopPercent`), clamped 20-80%, drag by pointer travel (a jump-to-cursor version broke double-click), arrow keys nudge 2%, hidden and ignored at <=600px. Verified in Playwright: drag, clamp, reload persistence, plain click doesn't move it, double-click reset (also clears storage), keyboard, mobile hides the handles. Pulse's colour tokens are fixed dark (not theme-driven), so there is no light variant to check.
**Pulse live account refresh, 2026-09-21 (Marcelo asked for a periodic refresh).** *In plain terms:* the Pulse numbers for Account Value, Available/Total/Reserved cash and the Allocation bar now refresh by themselves every minute while the tab is open, and immediately when you come back to the tab. Yesterday's P&L is untouched (still last night's snapshot). *Technical:* `/dashboard/available-cash` now also returns live `netLiquidationValue` (same single IBKR account-summary call, no extra load). Pulse polls it every 60 s while visible + on refocus, keeps last good values on failure. Account Value tile, cash % and the Allocation denominator use the live value; Cash slice = 100 - other slices (the server's 'unallocated' row is computed at stream open and goes stale). Unrealised-% base and Yesterday's P&L still use the nightly snapshot. *Timing (local, via tunnel):* `available-cash` 0.62-0.86 s, `account-value` (DB) 2 ms, one-shot `/exposure` 1.5-2.0 s, exposure stream first event 1.1-1.4 s -> 1/min is ~1.4% duty and 1 IBKR request/min against a 50 msg/s cap; not aggressive. Verified in Playwright: values changed on the 60 s poll, refocus fired an immediate fetch. Mobile Pulse clips the KPI tiles (fixed wall-display layout; not changed here).
**Prod verification + open list, 2026-09-20 (after Marcelo pushed and ran the SQL).** Release v196 (`24a4347`) live; migration batch 29 created `last_known_prices` (still empty right after deploy — fills as price screens are used; re-check after opening Positions on prod). Prod snapshot fix applied (stock/unrealized snapshot totals -44,954.85 / -3,335.84 = the dev result). Nightly `expiry_settlement_audit` ran 2026-09-19 22:00 UTC in `apply` mode: 19 corrections, realized P&L +4,385.06 (= 2,936.78 first set + 1,448.28 MU exit-price fix, as predicted). Commission backfill (110/110), AAOI/PLTR leg fixes and the HOOD-fills patch are all run on prod. **Still open:** (1) Monday market hours: greeks parity chain vs Positions; first live fill should populate `trades.commission` via the worker's `commissionReport` handler (deployed 2026-09-19, currency assumed USD — verify); watchdog's first check of the new job (Mon 22:30 UTC); (2) FROZEN fallback for the option chain (blank on a closed market); (3) Return on capital is per-handoff and not annualised (low priority); (4) the other session's connection-multiplexing work — rerun the direct IBKR frozen-price probe after it lands (price service sits above the connection layer, should be unaffected); (5) assignment-sale commissions (~USD 6, no trade rows) are not in P&L; (6) positions with several stock legs were not covered by the snapshot lag fix; (7) dividends in cycle P&L (actual IBKR cash transactions only, not the ex-dividend calendar) — not built.
**Price/greeks consistency audit + modal fix, 2026-09-19 (Marcelo: 'make the modal work like the positions table; check the whole platform').** *Trigger:* AAOI showed $104.90 on Positions but $98.06 in Ticker Detail. Root cause: the modal's pricing stream (live connection, REALTIME-only) gets no `last` on a closed market and only tick 9 `previousClose` (a session old); the header did `last ?? previousClose`. **Fixed (committed, not yet pushed):** `streamTickerDetail` now also runs `streamLivePrices` (the Positions source: frozen last, then live, last-trade only) and sends a `spot` event; the header, change %, chain strike selection and technicals use it; the modal never shows a previous close as a price; Positions' Price column now prefers the stock leg's live market value (same source as its P&L) before the option tick's underlying price. **Inventory of price paths:** (A) FROZEN->REALTIME, last-only: Positions P&L, Dashboard exposure/portfolio (`positionExposure`), Price Performance current prices, Trade Alerts current prices, modal header (now). (B) REALTIME pricing snapshot with `last ?? previousClose` fallback (stale outside hours): `fetchTickerQuoteSnapshot` (Genosuke), `generateTradeAlertCandidates`, `refreshTradeAlert`, `evaluateRecoveryPathForPosition`, `streamPositionQuote` (New Position form), and the modal's strike/technicals fallback. (C) Nightly P&L snapshot: `fetchDailyClosingPrices` (fixed 2026-09-18). (D) Option greeks: FROZEN->REALTIME model greeks (Positions, PositionCard, snapshot job) vs REALTIME-only chain quotes (`quoteOptionChain`: Ticker Detail chain, alerts, roll candidates, assignment-risk, order review) — blank outside market hours and never cross-checked against (A). **Findings:** (1) seven `pricing.last ?? previousClose` spots still read a session-old price outside market hours.
